# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Latency plan X1-B (audit O5): batches bounded by figure count
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def _weighted_items(*weights: int) -> list[ContextItem]:
    sentences = [f"Site {index:03d} reported its capacity figures." for index in range(len(weights))]
    read = make_read(" ".join(sentences), url="https://example.test/weights", title="Weights")
    return [
        _item(read, make_finding(
            read, sentence,
            figures=[figure(str(10 + n), "GW", "2025", "actual") for n in range(weight)],
            content=f"Site {index:03d} finding",
        ))
        for index, (sentence, weight) in enumerate(zip(sentences, weights))
    ]


def _weights(batches: list[list[ContextItem]]) -> list[list[int]]:
    return [[len(item.finding.figures) for item in batch] for batch in batches]


def test_without_a_figure_bound_batches_are_cut_by_count_alone() -> None:
    items = _weighted_items(1, 1, 1, 1, 1, 16, 1)

    assert _weights(context_check_batches(items, 5)) == [[1, 1, 1, 1, 1], [16, 1]]


def test_a_figure_bound_closes_a_batch_and_asks_a_heavy_finding_alone() -> None:
    items = _weighted_items(1, 16, 2, 2, 1, 9, 1, 1, 1, 1, 1)

    assert _weights(context_check_batches(items, 5, 12)) == [
        [1], [16], [2, 2, 1], [9, 1, 1, 1], [1, 1],
    ]


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_the_configured_figure_bound_reaches_the_context_check(tracker: Tracker) -> None:
    read = make_read(_metrics_page(4), url="https://example.test/batch", title="Batch metrics")
    findings = [_metric_finding(read, i) for i in range(4)]
    completer = ScriptedCompleter(outputs=[_confirm_reply] * 2)
    agent = _evidence_verifier(
        tracker, completer,
        config=AgentRuntimeConfig(verifier_batch_size=5, verifier_batch_figures=2),
    )
    state = _state(raw_findings=findings, read_records={read.read_id: read})

    async with tracker.session_span("session-1", "question"):
        await agent.run(state)

    assert [call[0] for call in completer.calls] == ["ContextCheckDraft"] * 2
````

CONTEXT_CHECK_CONCURRENCY = 8

def context_check_batches(
    items: Sequence["ContextItem"], size: int, figures: int | None = None
) -> list[list["ContextItem"]]:
    """The Context Check's batches, in the items' own order.

    At most ``size`` items each; with ``figures`` (latency plan X1-B), also at
    most that many figures each, a finding with more of them in a batch of its
    own. Without it, exactly the count-only cut the verifier always made.
    """
    if figures is None:
        return [list(items[i : i + size]) for i in range(0, len(items), size)]
    batches: list[list[ContextItem]] = []
    current: list[ContextItem] = []
    carried = 0
    for item in items:
        weight = len(item.finding.figures)
        if current and (len(current) == size or carried + weight > figures):
            batches.append(current)
            current, carried = [], 0
        current.append(item)
        carried += weight
    if current:
        batches.append(current)
    return batches

CONTEXT_CHECK_SYSTEM_PROMPT = (
````

### Task 24: X3 — the planner at `high` (suite gate, paired runs, keep or revert)

**What changes.** The planner's `reasoning_effort` goes from `max` to `high` (`config.yaml:32-34`).

**The risk.** The planner decides coverage, so it is the most accuracy-sensitive change, and the last (H3). Both tiers are as in Task 23. The suite compares `--reasoning-effort max` (control) with `high` (treatment), so the control does not depend on the evaluation block's own default.

**If kept**, one line in `config.yaml`, and the three places that state the shipped planner effort. Planning checked that no replay row moves with it.

**Files:**
- Modify (only if kept): `config.yaml` (line 33), `tests/test_config.py` (`test_the_shipped_llm_block_declares_the_measured_agent_efforts`, `test_the_shipped_per_agent_efforts_are_supported_by_the_provider`), `scripts/native_react_shape_probe.py` (line 107), `tests/test_native_react_shape_probe.py` (line 758), and the record.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `--reasoning-effort` (`src/deep_research/evaluation/cli.py`); Task 17's commands; the baseline runs.
- Produces: the decision, recorded, and a config commit only if kept.

**The arm's override** (P14). The planner entry is always:

```
"planner": {"reasoning_effort": "high", "timeout": 1800.0}
```

Add the pins of the earlier decisions:
- If X2 was kept, add `"researcher": {"reasoning_effort": "high", "timeout": 1800.0}` inside `model_overrides`. A replaced entry drops `thinking_mode`, so the researcher falls back to the global `enabled` (`config.yaml:10`).
- If X1-A was kept, add `"agents": {"verifier_batch_size": 5}` at the top level.
- If X1-B was kept, add `"agents": {"verifier_batch_figures": null}` at the top level instead.

## Acceptance criteria and where each is met

| # | Criterion (each is a test or a recorded command) | Met by |
|---|---|---|
| AC1 | Every request and the event-type order of all 35 replay rows are pinned before any change, and are unchanged by Tasks 2–7, 9–11, 13–14, 16–19 | Task 1; each task's "Prove nothing else moved" or re-pin step |
| AC2 | O9 moves only the three rows whose script reads on its forced turn. A model that obeys the last-turn instruction loses only the forced turns' requests and keeps the event order, on all 35 rows | Task 8, `test_skipping_the_forced_final_turn_drops_only_that_turns_requests` |
| AC3 | O4 moves only the `full` request digest of 22 rows. `timing_free`, `outside_research`, counts and event order are unchanged | Task 12 Step 5 (the re-pinned values and the comment above them) |
| AC4 | The replay matrix accepts 35/35 rows, three repetitions, deterministic, zero network, before and after Part 1 | Task 1 Step 4, Task 15 Step 2 |
| AC5 | No file under `web/` and no prompt changes; the web unit tests pass | Task 15 Step 3 |
| AC6 | `researcher.tool_call` carries `lock_wait_s` and `duration_s`; `researcher.sub_topic.completed` carries the tail split; every provider call has a labelled record with tokens | Tasks 2, 3, 4 |
| AC7 | `agents.verifier_concurrency` is 64 | Task 5 |
| AC8 | Finalize writes all cited findings in one `save_many`, falls back per finding on failure, and builds the ONNX model once per embedding provider | Task 6 |
| AC9 | Owed batches, failed halves and reputation lookups run together, and their results are applied in their original order | Tasks 7, 9, 11 |
| AC10 | No request is made for a `.doc`, `.docx`, `.xls` or `.xlsx` source | Task 10 |
| AC11 | One page is downloaded once; two pages and searches overlap; decisions and commits never interleave; D9's amendment is in the spec and the historical plan | Task 12 |
| AC12 | One connection pool per run (none with a proxy), closed with the run; each call keeps its own client and cookies; robots.txt once per host, failed fetches retried | Tasks 13, 14 |
| AC13 | The stage capture writes nothing unless bound | Task 16 (tests and the unchanged guard) |
| AC14 | The paired-run verdicts, the tier-3 gate, the stage-replay rule and the peak-hour refusal are code with tests, written before any paid run | Tasks 17, 18 |
| AC15 | The harness can run one agent's target with thinking disabled, with the judge unchanged and the run labelled an experiment | Task 19 |
| AC16 | Each experiment has a checkpoint before its first paid call, an exact protocol, accuracy-first criteria, and a recorded keep or revert. A rejected experiment leaves no config and no code it needed | Tasks 20–25 |

## Test plan

| Layer | What | Where |
|---|---|---|
| Unit | Each change's own behaviour: overlap tests with held replies (`HoldingCompleter`), order tests, failure and cancellation tests, cache semantics | each task's Step 1 |
| Contract | The agent pins: a pin moves only in the task that edits that module | `tests/test_evaluation/test_config.py` |
| Whole graph, offline | Every request and the event order of all 35 replay rows; two whole notes replays; the planner's reader packets | `tests/test_e2e_evaluation/test_request_digests.py`, `tests/test_graph/test_reader_notes_replay.py`, `tests/test_agents/test_planner_reader_answers.py` |
| Matrix, offline | `python -m deep_research.e2e_evaluation suite --tier controlled --repetitions 3`: every row accepted, deterministic, zero network | Tasks 1, 15, and every kept config change |
| Full suite | about 5,000 tests, with placeholder secrets | Tasks 1, 15, 25 |
| Web | Vitest, unchanged | Task 15 |
| Stage replay, paid | Two captures, three plus three repetitions per experiment | Tasks 21, 22 |
| Per-agent harness, paid | The verifier's live case; the researcher's and the planner's controlled suites, two arms each | Tasks 21–24 |
| Paired live runs, paid | Four baseline runs, three per tier-4 experiment | Tasks 20, 23, 24 |

## Self-review

1. **Spec coverage.**
   - Every approved item is a task: O8 is Tasks 2–4, O1 is 5, O2 is 6, O3 is 7, O9 is 8, O10 is 9, O11 is 10, O12 is 11, and O4 is 12–14.
   - D9's amendment is Task 12.
   - X1 is Tasks 18 and 21–22, X2 is Tasks 19 and 23, and X3 is Task 24, each behind its own checkpoint and ending in a decision.
   - The audit's tier-2 tool is Tasks 16 and 18, the harness toggle is Task 19, and the paired-run protocol is Tasks 17 and 20.
   - The compatibility section covers the four named overlaps, plus `graph/nodes.py`, `main.py`, event metadata and the pins.
   - Nothing on the skip list is planned.
   - The audit's fourth O4 part, intra-turn concurrency, is not approved, and is Open issue OI-1.
2. **Placeholder scan.**
   - No "TBD", "TODO" or "similar to".
   - Every code step has its code.
   - Every command block has an Expected line. For the paid steps, the Expected line is the outcome the protocol defines. The plan cannot know their values, so the commands that need a run-time value find it themselves: Task 23 Step 3 lists the newest `results.json` per prefix.
3. **Type and name consistency.** The names each task's Interfaces block produces are the ones later tasks consume:
   - `ToolTimingCallback`, `ExtractionTimings`, `call_label`, `CallRecordTelemetry`, `gather_or_cancel`, `HoldingCompleter`;
   - `SKIP_FINAL_ANSWER_TURN`, `ToolGate`, `flight_key`, `SharedConnectionPool`;
   - `bind_stage_capture`, `capture_node_input`, `capture_statement_check`;
   - `peak_ahead`, `run_one`, `compare`, `suite_verdict`, `replay_capture`, `summarize`;
   - `target_thinking_mode`, `context_check_batches`, `verifier_batch_figures`.

   The executed run of this document, which applied every block in order, checks them.
4. **Compatibility with the notes branch.**
   - Every agent re-pin is the B-pin command, which reads the value in place (Task 1's helper), so a re-pin applies in either merge order.
   - Task 12 changes only the lock's own lines in `researcher.py`. The `ToolGate` is an `asyncio.Lock`, so no signature that passes it changes.
   - The Compatibility section lists every other overlap and how the second branch to merge resolves it.
